+private blob directories are retired by a separately
          authorized, recoverable
    569 +cleanup operation.
    570 +
    571 +## 13. Implementation order
    572 +
    573 +1. Add the V2 relay contract and explicit public end
         point configuration.
    574 +2. Add safe global PostgreSQL migrations and models.
    575 +3. Implement Laravel enrollment, ownership, pairing,
          operation, blob, lease,
    576 +   outbox, and policy repositories.
    577 +4. Generalize the existing realtime outbox publisher
          for relay transitions.
    578 +5. Extract the Pycore transport-neutral RPC executio
         n kernel and route policy.
    579 +6. Replace the Pycore relay runtime with enrollment,
          wake-plus-claim, byte
    580 +   response, reconciliation, and operation-ledger be
         havior.
    581 +7. Replace UI target selection and relay delivery be
         hind `PycoreTransport`.
    582 +8. Add one UI Mercure runtime for roster and operati
         on state, with cursor-loss
    583 +   reconciliation.
    584 +9. Refactor Terminal screenshot metadata/resources a
         nd rendering cadence.
    585 +10. Update the minimum-step shell/Caddy/web-access p
         rovisioning.
    586 +11. Remove V1 routes, classes, contract entries, sto
         rage keys, and hardcoded
    587 +    relay strings in the same cutover.
    588 +12. Perform source, deployment, and live acceptance
         checks only when explicitly
    589 +    authorized.
    590 +
    591 +## 14. Acceptance contract
    592 +
    593 +The design is complete only when all of these statem
         ents are true:
    594 +
    595 +- A logged-in `ui12gm.com` user sees only owned devi
         ces.
    596 +- A private Pycore with outbound Internet access can
          enroll, appear online,
    597 +  pair, and execute an allowed operation without an
         inbound port.
    598 +- Port 59000 remains unreachable from the public Int
         ernet.
    599 +- Lost and duplicate Mercure updates neither lose no
         r duplicate operations.
    600 +- Duplicate UI admission returns the same operation;
          a changed body under the
    601 +  same key returns conflict.
    602 +- Concurrent Laravel workers cannot lease the same o
         peration.
    603 +- A Pycore restart resumes safe operations and never
          auto-replays an ambiguous
    604 +  at-most-once desktop action.
    605 +- JSON, text, images, audio, empty bodies, and non-2
         xx responses preserve exact
    606 +  status, headers, length, and SHA-256 through the r
         elay.
    607 +- An unauthorized user cannot list, pair, invoke, in
         spect, or download another
    608 +  user's device data.
    609 +- Revoking a pairing prevents new operations immedia
         tely; short-lived Mercure
    610 +  credentials expire without granting data-plane acc
         ess.
    611 +- A hub outage increases latency but does not lose a
         ccepted operations.
    612 +- Laravel request workers are not held by relay long
          polling.
    613 +- Terminal transmits no Base64 screenshots in snapsh
         ot JSON and does not
    614 +  recapture unchanged invisible windows.
    615 +- `ui12gm.com` reads `api.si.12gm.com` from runtime
         configuration, never from
    616 +  hostname inference.
    617 +- Every installer/provisioner step can repair its ow
         n missing artifact even
    618 +  when earlier artifacts already exist.
    619 +
    620 +## 15. Official sources
    621 +
    622 +- [Mercure specification](https://mercure.rocks/spec
         ) — private updates,
    623 +  authorization, reconciliation, topic matching, and
          SSE data requirements.
    624 +- [Mercure subscribing guide](https://github.com/dun
         glas/mercure/blob/main/docs/concepts/subscribing.md)
    625 +  — authenticated fetch streams, one multiplexed con
         nection, and current
    626 +  matcher behavior.
    627 +- [FrankenPHP Mercure](https://frankenphp.dev/docs/m
         ercure/) — built-in hub,
    628 +  Caddy configuration, and `mercure_publish()`.
    629 +- [FrankenPHP Laravel integration](https://frankenph
         p.dev/docs/laravel/) —
    630 +  Octane/FrankenPHP worker mode and Mercure integrat
         ion.
    631 +- [FrankenPHP v1.12.7 release](https://github.com/ph
         p/frankenphp/releases/tag/v1.12.7)
    632 +  — runtime fixes and embedded dependency updates.
    633 +- [Laravel 13 query builder](https://laravel.com/doc
         s/13.x/queries) — database
    634 +  transactions and pessimistic locking.
    635 +- [Laravel Octane](https://laravel.com/docs/12.x/oct
         ane) — worker lifetime,
    636 +  concurrency, and request execution limits.
    637 +- [PostgreSQL `SELECT`](https://www.postgresql.org/d
         ocs/current/sql-select.html)
    638 +  — row locks and `SKIP LOCKED` queue-style claiming
         .
    639 +- [PostgreSQL transaction isolation](https://www.pos
         tgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html)
    640 +  — concurrent updates and `ON CONFLICT` behavior.
    641 +- [WHATWG Server-Sent Events](https://html.spec.what
         wg.org/multipage/server-sent-events.html)
    642 +  — SSE parsing, reconnection, and `Last-Event-ID`.
    645 +  requests.

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

• 设计文档已完成：docs_fix/
  DESIGN_20260823_PYCORE_REMOTE_RELAY_V2.md

  核心结论：

  - 远程链路改为 ui12gm.com → api.si.12gm.com → 私网 Pycore，
    Pycore 全程主动出站，不开放 59000。

  - Mercure 只负责唤醒和状态通知；PostgreSQL、事务 Outbox
    和私有 Blob 存储才是数据真相，符合 Mercure 规范
    (https://mercure.rocks/spec)及 PostgreSQL 的 SKIP LOCKED

    (https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-select.html)并发领取模型。

  - 不做薄兼容代理：抽取 Pycore 通用 RPC 执行内核，直连与
    Relay 共用执行、权限、超时、字节响应和幂等逻辑。

  - UI 明确配置 https://api.si.12gm.com，禁止继续根据
    ui12gm.com 推导 API 域名。

  - 设备采用用户确认的一次性注册和非对称签名，所有设备、配
    对、操作、文件均绑定所有者。

  - 操作采用稳定 ID、请求摘要、原子领取、执行账本及事务
    Outbox；鼠标键盘等不可安全重放的操作进入
    execution_unknown，不会虚假宣称 exactly-once。

  - 请求和响应按原始字节传输，解决现有 .text/UTF-8 转换导致图
    片、音频等二进制损坏的问题。

  - 安装与配置幂等拆为最小步骤，不使用总入口哨兵，也不使用
    Shell 返回码作为业务返回值。

  源码审计还发现了现有 V1 Relay 的严重问题：配对过期字段不一
  致、设备在线字段不一致、全局配对覆盖、缓存并发竞争、Mercure
  发布失败会遗失唤醒、PHP 最长约 25 秒轮询占用工作线程，以及
  重复事件可能重复执行本地操作。

  当前 UI/Pycore 卡顿最明显的代码级原因是 Terminal：每两秒抓
  取全部窗口、PNG 编码后转 Base64 放入 JSON，同时页面时钟可能
  导致整棵窗口树频繁重绘。文档已要求从领域层改成“元数据 + 按
  需二进制截图 + ETag + 可见窗口捕获 + 变更事件”，而不是在
  Relay 层压缩补救。

  本轮只新增设计文档，没有修改业务代码，也没有运行构建、测试
  或服务。官方依据已经收录在文档末尾，包括 FrankenPHP Mercure
  (https://frankenphp.dev/docs/mercure/)、FrankenPHP Laravel
t-methods)。

─ Worked for 14m 24s ────────────────────────────────────────

  档中更新进度，注意主文档指明几个进度副 文档后不要再修改，
  不要改php等代码，并在文档中指明并行AI看文档和进度在另一端
  修改php，注意从底层构架，不要薄兼容，代码从底层重构，同时
  搜索官方文档，按规范修改，shell不要使用返回码退出值、信任
  上一个函数的运行结果。同时需要有幂等性，幂等需要拆为最小
  步、而不是从一大个入口就幂等跳过。
  复用重复实现类库。同时搜索官方文档，搜索官方文档。e
  pycore端要增强大量的日志显示详继类库，所有行为都要调用中心
  日志类库打印，按pycore的规范修改代码。

    AI 交接副文档，再审计、查官方规范、底层重构并记录进度。不
    会修改 PHP、Laravel、UI、Shell 或测试。

    28 +    @staticmethod
    29 +    def _safe_value(key: str, value: Any) -> str:
    30 +        normalized_key = str(key or "").lower()
    31 +        if any(part in normalized_key for part in REL
        AY_LOG_SENSITIVE_PARTS):
    32 +            return RELAY_LOG_REDACTED
    33 +        if isinstance(value, bytes):
    34 +            digest = hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest(
        )
    35 +            return f"bytes:{len(value)} sha256:{diges
        t}"
    36 +        if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
    37 +            return f"items:{len(value)}"
    38 +        if isinstance(value, dict):

    40 +        text = str(value).replace("\r", "\\r").replac
        e("\n", "\\n")
    41 +        if len(text) > RELAY_LOG_VALUE_LIMIT:
    42 +            return text[:RELAY_LOG_VALUE_LIMIT] + "..
        ."

        y]) -> str:
    47 +        fields = [f"action={str(action or 'unknown')}
        "]
    48 +        fields.extend(
    49 +            f"{key}={cls._safe_value(key, context[key
        ])}"
    50 +            for key in sorted(context)
    51 +            if context[key] is not None and context[k
        ey] != ""
    52 +        )
    53 +        return f"[{RELAY_LOG_COMPONENT}] " + " ".join
        (fields)
    54 +
    55 +    def debug(self, action: str, **context: Any) -> N

    57 +

         None:

    63 +
    64 +    def warning(self, action: str, **context: Any) ->
         None:
    65 +        ColorPrint.yellow(self._line(action, context)

    66 +
    67 +    def error(self, action: str, **context: Any) -> N

     35 +        super().__init__(str(code))
     36 +        self.code = str(code)
     37 +        self.status_code = int(status_code)
     38 +
     39 +
     40 +class RpcExecutionKernel:
     41 +    """One route table and execution pipeline shared
          by HTTP and Relay."""
     42 +
     43 +    def __init__(self) -> None:
     44 +        self.dispatcher = HttpDispatcher(sync_invoke
         r=self._invoke_sync_handler)
     45 +
     46 +    @staticmethod
     47 +    async def _invoke_sync_handler(handler: Any, arg
         uments: tuple) -> Any:
     48 +        return await await_bus_task(
     49 +            handler,
     50 +            *arguments,
     51 +            thread_name="RpcRouteThread",
     52 +        )
     53 +
     54 +    def register(
     55 +        self,
     56 +        path: str,
     57 +        handler: Any,
     58 +        **options: Any,
     59 +    ) -> HttpRoute:
     60 +        return self.dispatcher.register(path, handle
         r, **options)
     61 +
     62 +    async def dispatch(
     63 +        self,
     64 +        route: HttpRoute,
     65 +        params: Dict[str, Any],
     66 +        request_id: str,
     67 +        context: Dict[str, Any],
     68 +    ) -> Any:
     69 +        return await self.dispatcher.dispatch(route,
          params, request_id, context)
     70 +
     71 +    def decode_request_params(
     72 +        self,
     73 +        method: str,
     74 +        query: Mapping[str, Any],
     75 +        body: bytes,
     76 +        content_type: str,
     77 +    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
     78 +        params = self._query_params(query)
     79 +        if str(method or "GET").upper() == "GET" or
         not body:
     80 +            return params
     81 +        normalized_type = str(content_type or "").lo
         wer()
     82 +        if normalized_type.startswith(RPC_TEXT_CONTE
         NT_TYPE):
     83 +            params["text"] = body.decode("utf-8", er
         rors="replace")
     84 +            return params
     85 +        payload = json.loads(body.decode("utf-8"))
     86 +        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
     87 +            raise RpcExecutionError("request_body_no
         t_object", 400)
     88 +        return {**params, **payload}
     89 +
     90 +    async def execute_relay_async(
     91 +        self,
     92 +        method: str,
     93 +        path: str,
     94 +        query: Mapping[str, Any],
     95 +        headers: Mapping[str, Any],
     96 +        body: bytes,
     97 +        operation_id: str,
     98 +        pairing_id: str,
     99 +        user_id: str,
    100 +    ) -> RpcExecutionResponse:
    101 +        route_path = self.route_path(path)
    102 +        normalized_method = str(method or "GET").upp
         er()
    103 +        route = self.dispatcher.get(route_path)
    104 +        if route is None:
    105 +            raise RpcExecutionError("route_not_found
         ", 404)
    106 +        if normalized_method not in route.methods:
    107 +            raise RpcExecutionError("method_not_allo
         wed", 405)
    108 +        policy = relay_contract.route_policy(route_p
         ath, normalized_method)
    109 +        if str(policy.get("exposure") or "denied") !
         = "relay":
    110 +            raise RpcExecutionError("route_relay_den
         ied", 403)
    111 +        content_type = self._header(headers, "conten
         t-type")
    112 +        params = self.decode_request_params(
    113 +            normalized_method,
    114 +            query,
    115 +            body,
    116 +            content_type,
    117 +        )
    118 +        request_id = str(operation_id or uuid.uuid4(
         ).hex)
    119 +        context = {
    120 +            "transport": "relay",
    121 +            "request_id": request_id,
    122 +            "operation_id": operation_id,
    123 +            "pairing_id": pairing_id,
    124 +            "user_id": user_id,
    125 +            "client_id": pairing_id,
    126 +            "browser_id": pairing_id,
    127 +            "remote_addr": None,
    128 +            "user_agent": self._header(headers, "use
         r-agent"),
    129 +            "method": normalized_method,
    130 +            "path": route_path,
    131 +            "path_params": {},
    132 +            "headers": self.filtered_headers(headers
         , "request"),
    133 +            "relay_policy": dict(policy),
    134 +        }
    135 +        relay_activity_log.info(
    136 +            "rpc.dispatch.started",
    137 +            operation_id=operation_id,
    138 +            method=normalized_method,
    139 +            route=route_path,
    140 +            retry_policy=policy.get("retry"),
    141 +            body_length=len(body),
    142 +        )
    143 +        result = await self.dispatch(route, params,
         request_id, context)
    144 +        response = self.encode_result(result, reques
         t_id, filter_for_relay=True)
    145 +        relay_activity_log.success(
    146 +            "rpc.dispatch.completed",
    147 +            operation_id=operation_id,
    148 +            method=normalized_method,
    149 +            route=route_path,
    150 +            status=response.status_code,
    151 +            body=response.body,
    152 +        )
    153 +        return response
    154 +
    155 +    def execute_relay(
    156 +        self,
    157 +        method: str,
    158 +        path: str,
    159 +        query: Mapping[str, Any],
    160 +        headers: Mapping[str, Any],
    161 +        body: bytes,
    162 +        operation_id: str,
    163 +        pairing_id: str,
    164 +        user_id: str,
    165 +    ) -> RpcExecutionResponse:
    166 +        return asyncio.run(
    167 +            self.execute_relay_async(
    168 +                method,
    169 +                path,
    170 +                query,
    171 +                headers,
    172 +                body,
    173 +                operation_id,
    174 +                pairing_id,
    175 +                user_id,
    176 +            )
    177 +        )
    178 +
    179 +    def encode_result(
    180 +        self,
    181 +        result: Any,
    182 +        request_id: str,
    183 +        filter_for_relay: bool = False,
    184 +    ) -> RpcExecutionResponse:
    185 +        if result is None:
    186 +            response = RpcExecutionResponse(204, {},
          b"")
    187 +        elif hasattr(result, "status_code") and hasa
         ttr(result, "body"):
    188 +            response = RpcExecutionResponse(
    189 +                int(getattr(result, "status_code")),
    190 +                {
    191 +                    str(key): str(value)
    192 +                    for key, value in dict(getattr(r
         esult, "headers", {}) or {}).items()
    193 +                },
    194 +                bytes(getattr(result, "body") or b""
         ),
    195 +            )
    196 +        else:
    197 +            fastapi = get_third_package_fastapi()
    198 +            encoded = fastapi.encoders.jsonable_enco
         der(result)
    199 +            body = json.dumps(
    200 +                encoded,
    201 +                ensure_ascii=False,
    202 +                allow_nan=False,
    203 +                separators=(",", ":"),
    204 +            ).encode("utf-8")
    205 +            response = RpcExecutionResponse(
    206 +                200,
    207 +                {"Content-Type": RPC_JSON_CONTENT_TY
         PE},
    208 +                body,
    209 +            )
    210 +        headers = dict(response.headers)
    211 +        headers["X-Request-ID"] = str(request_id)
    212 +        if filter_for_relay:
    213 +            headers = self.filtered_headers(headers,
          "response")
    214 +        return RpcExecutionResponse(response.status_
         code, headers, response.body)
    215 +
    216 +    @staticmethod
    217 +    def error_response(
    218 +        code: str,

          "/"):

         ct(query or {}).items()}

    253 +    @staticmethod

         r) -> str:
    255 +        normalized_name = str(name).lower()
    256 +        for key, value in dict(headers or {}).items(
         ):
    257 +            if str(key).lower() == normalized_name:
    258 +                return str(value)
    259 +        return ""

         ytes")

         ems()

    272 +            and len(str(value).encode("utf-8")) <= l
         imit
    273 +        }

      1 +# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
      2 +"""Signed outbound-only Laravel transport for the Py
         core Relay V2 agent."""
      3 +
      4 +from __future__ import annotations
      5 +
      6 +import hashlib
      7 +import json
      8 +import time
      9 +from typing import Any, Dict, Mapping, Optional
     10 +
     11 +from pycore.pyutils.common.relay_activity_log import
          relay_activity_log
     12 +from pycore.pyutils.common.relay_contract import rel
         ay_contract
     13 +from pycore.pyutils.common.relay_identity import rel
         ay_device_identity
     14 +from pycore.pyutils.laravel.client import laravel_cl
         ient
     15 +from pycore.pyutils.laravel.endpoint_manager import
         laravel_endpoint_manager
     16 +
     17 +
     18 +RELAY_JSON_CONTENT_TYPE = "application/json"
     19 +RELAY_BINARY_CONTENT_TYPE = "application/octet-strea
         m"
     20 +
     21 +
     22 +class RelayHttpError(RuntimeError):
     23 +    """One coordinator response outside the successf
         ul HTTP range."""
     24 +
     25 +    def __init__(self, status_code: int, action: str
         ) -> None:
     26 +        super().__init__(f"relay_http_{status_code}:
         {action}")
     27 +        self.status_code = int(status_code)
     28 +        self.action = str(action)
     29 +
     30 +
     31 +class RelayTransport:
     32 +    """Encode exact request bytes, sign them, and re
         tain raw response bytes."""
     33 +
     34 +    @staticmethod
     35 +    def endpoint() -> str:
     36 +        return str(laravel_endpoint_manager.get_acti
         ve_base_url() or "").rstrip("/")
     37 +
     38 +    def request_json(
     39 +        self,
     40 +        method: str,
     41 +        path: str,
     42 +        payload: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
     43 +        query: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
     44 +        timeout: Optional[float] = None,
     45 +        action: str = "coordinator.request",
     46 +    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
     47 +        body = (
     48 +            json.dumps(
     49 +                dict(payload),
     50 +                ensure_ascii=False,
     51 +                allow_nan=False,
     52 +                sort_keys=True,
     53 +                separators=(",", ":"),
     54 +            ).encode("utf-8")
     55 +            if payload is not None
     56 +            else b""
     57 +        )
     58 +        response = self._request(
     59 +            method,
     60 +            path,
     61 +            query or {},
     62 +            body,
     63 +            RELAY_JSON_CONTENT_TYPE,
     64 +            timeout,
     65 +            action,
     66 +        )
     67 +        data = response.json()
     68 +        if not isinstance(data, dict):
     69 +            raise TypeError("relay_response_root_not
         _object")
     70 +        nested = data.get("data")
     71 +        return dict(nested) if isinstance(nested, di
         ct) else dict(data)
     72 +
     73 +    def request_bytes(
     74 +        self,
     75 +        method: str,
     76 +        path: str,
     77 +        body: bytes = b"",
     78 +        query: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
     79 +        content_type: str = RELAY_BINARY_CONTENT_TYP
         E,
     80 +        timeout: Optional[float] = None,
     81 +        action: str = "coordinator.bytes",
     82 +    ) -> Any:
     83 +        return self._request(
     84 +            method,
     85 +            path,
     86 +            query or {},
     87 +            bytes(body),
     88 +            content_type,
     89 +            timeout,
     90 +            action,
     91 +        )
     92 +
     93 +    def _request(
     94 +        self,
     95 +        method: str,
     96 +        path: str,
     97 +        query: Mapping[str, Any],
     98 +        body: bytes,
     99 +        content_type: str,
    100 +        timeout: Optional[float],
    101 +        action: str,
    102 +    ) -> Any:
    103 +        endpoint = self.endpoint()
    104 +        if not endpoint:
    105 +            raise RuntimeError("relay_coordinator_en
         dpoint_unavailable")
    106 +        normalized_method = str(method or "GET").upp
         er()
    107 +        headers = relay_device_identity.signed_heade
         rs(
    108 +            normalized_method,
    109 +            path,
    110 +            query,
    111 +            body,
    112 +        )
    113 +        if body or normalized_method != "GET":
    114 +            headers["Content-Type"] = str(content_ty
         pe)
    115 +        started = time.perf_counter()
    116 +        relay_activity_log.info(
    117 +            action + ".started",
    118 +            method=normalized_method,
    119 +            path=path,
    120 +            query=dict(query),
    121 +            body=body,
    122 +            endpoint=endpoint,
    123 +        )
    124 +        response = laravel_client.request(
    125 +            normalized_method,
    126 +            path,
    127 +            base_url=endpoint,
    128 +            params=dict(query),
    129 +            data=body if body else None,
    130 +            headers=headers,
    131 +            timeout=(
    132 +                relay_contract.duration("request_tim
         eout_seconds")
    133 +                if timeout is None
    134 +                else float(timeout)
    135 +            ),
    136 +            allow_redirects=False,
    137 +        )
    138 +        status = int(getattr(response, "status_code"
         , 0) or 0)
    139 +        elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started)
          * 1000
    140 +        if status < 200 or status >= 300:
    141 +            relay_activity_log.error(
    142 +                action + ".failed",
    143 +                method=normalized_method,
    144 +                path=path,
    145 +                status=status,
    146 +                duration_ms=f"{elapsed_ms:.1f}",
    147 +            )
    148 +            raise RelayHttpError(status, action)
    149 +        content = bytes(getattr(response, "content",
          b"") or b"")
    150 +        relay_activity_log.success(
    151 +            action + ".completed",
    152 +            method=normalized_method,
    153 +            path=path,
    154 +            status=status,
    155 +            duration_ms=f"{elapsed_ms:.1f}",
    156 +            response_length=len(content),
    157 +            response_sha256=hashlib.sha256(content).
         hexdigest(),
    158 +        )
    159 +        return response
    160 +
    161 +
    162 +relay_transport = RelayTransport()
    163 +
    164 +
    165 +__all__ = ["RelayHttpError", "relay_transport"]

      1 +# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
      2 +"""Claimed Relay operation validation, in-process ex

      3 +
      4 +from __future__ import annotations
      5 +
      6 +import base64

         port (
     15 +    RELAY_EXECUTE,
     16 +    RELAY_EXECUTION_UNKNOWN,
     17 +    RELAY_REPLAY_RESPONSE,
     18 +    relay_execution_ledger,
     19 +)
     20 +from pycore.pyutils.common.relay_identity import rel
         ay_device_identity

     22 +    RpcExecutionError,

     26 +
     27 +
     28 +RELAY_RESULT_RESPONDED = "responded"
     29 +RELAY_RESULT_FAILED = "failed"
     30 +
     31 +
     32 +class RelayOperationProcessor:
     33 +    """Process each operation as independently retry
         able protocol steps."""
     34 +
     35 +    def process_many(self, operations: Iterable[Mapp
         ing[str, Any]]) -> None:
     36 +        for descriptor in operations:
     37 +            operation_id = str(descriptor.get("opera
         tion_id") or "")
     38 +            try:
     39 +                self.process(descriptor)
     40 +            except Exception as exc:
     41 +                relay_activity_log.error(
     42 +                    "operation.processing.interrupte
         d",
     43 +                    operation_id=operation_id,
     44 +                    error_type=type(exc).__name__,
     45 +                    error=exc,
     46 +                )
     47 +
     48 +    def process(self, descriptor: Mapping[str, Any])
          -> None:
     49 +        request = self._validated_request(descriptor
         )
     50 +        operation_id = request["operation_id"]
     51 +        ledger = relay_execution_ledger.admit(
     52 +            operation_id,
     53 +            relay_device_identity.device_id(),
     54 +            request["request_digest"],
     55 +            request["route"],
     56 +            request["retry_policy"],
     57 +        )
     58 +        action = str(ledger["action"])
     59 +        if action == RELAY_REPLAY_RESPONSE:
     60 +            response = relay_execution_ledger.respon
         se(ledger["result"])
     61 +            self._post_response(request, response, R
         ELAY_RESULT_RESPONDED)
     62 +            return
     63 +        if action == RELAY_EXECUTION_UNKNOWN:
     64 +            self._post_execution_unknown(request, "p
         rior_execution_incomplete")
     65 +            return
     66 +        if action != RELAY_EXECUTE:
     67 +            raise RuntimeError("relay_ledger_action_
         invalid")
     68 +        self._execute(request)
     69 +
     70 +    def _execute(self, request: Dict[str, Any]) -> N
         one:
     71 +        operation_id = request["operation_id"]
     72 +        try:
     73 +            response = rpc_execution_kernel.execute_
         relay(
     74 +                request["method"],
     75 +                request["path"],
     76 +                request["query"],
     77 +                request["headers"],
     78 +                request["body"],
     79 +                operation_id,
     80 +                request["pairing_id"],
     81 +                request["user_id"],
     82 +            )
     83 +        except RpcExecutionError as exc:
     84 +            response = rpc_execution_kernel.error_re
         sponse(
     85 +                exc.code,
     86 +                exc.status_code,
     87 +                operation_id,
     88 +            )
     89 +            relay_execution_ledger.save_response(
     90 +                operation_id,
     91 +                request["request_digest"],
     92 +                response,
     93 +                failed=True,
     94 +            )
     95 +            self._post_response(request, response, R
         ELAY_RESULT_FAILED)
     96 +            return
     97 +        except TimeoutError:
     98 +            relay_execution_ledger.mark_unknown(
     99 +                operation_id,
    100 +                "rpc_execution_timeout",
    101 +            )
    102 +            self._post_execution_unknown(request, "r
         pc_execution_timeout")
    103 +            return
    104 +        except Exception as exc:
    105 +            relay_activity_log.error(
    106 +                "rpc.dispatch.failed",
    107 +                operation_id=operation_id,
    108 +                error_type=type(exc).__name__,
    109 +                error=exc,
    110 +            )
    111 +            response = rpc_execution_kernel.error_re
         sponse(
    112 +                "rpc_execution_failed",
    113 +                500,
    114 +                operation_id,
    115 +            )
    116 +            relay_execution_ledger.save_response(
    117 +                operation_id,
    118 +                request["request_digest"],
    119 +                response,
    120 +                failed=True,
    121 +            )
    122 +            self._post_response(request, response, R
         ELAY_RESULT_FAILED)
    123 +            return
    124 +        relay_execution_ledger.save_response(
    125 +            operation_id,
    126 +            request["request_digest"],
    127 +            response,
    128 +        )
    129 +        self._post_response(request, response, RELAY
         _RESULT_RESPONDED)
    130 +
    131 +    def _validated_request(
    132 +        self,
    133 +        descriptor: Mapping[str, Any],
    134 +    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    135 +        operation_id = str(descriptor.get("operation
         _id") or "")
    136 +        pairing_id = str(descriptor.get("pairing_id"
         ) or "")
    137 +        user_id = str(descriptor.get("user_id") or "
         ")
    138 +        method = str(descriptor.get("method") or "GE
         T").upper()
    139 +        path = str(descriptor.get("path") or "")
    140 +        query = {
    141 +            str(key): value
    142 +            for key, value in dict(descriptor.get("q
         uery") or {}).items()
    143 +        }
    144 +        headers = rpc_execution_kernel.filtered_head
         ers(
    145 +            dict(descriptor.get("headers") or {}),
    146 +            "request",
    147 +        )
    148 +        if not operation_id or not pairing_id or not
          user_id or not path:
    149 +            raise ValueError("relay_operation_descri
         ptor_incomplete")
    150 +        route = rpc_execution_kernel.route_path(path
         )
    151 +        policy = relay_contract.route_policy(route,
         method)
    152 +        if str(policy.get("exposure") or "denied") !
         = "relay":
    153 +            raise ValueError("relay_operation_route_
         denied")
    154 +        retry_policy = str(policy.get("retry") or "a
         t_most_once_action")
    155 +        body = self._request_body(descriptor, operat
         ion_id)
    156 +        body_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(body).hexdigest
         ()
    157 +        expected_body_sha256 = str(descriptor.get("b
         ody_sha256") or "")
    158 +        expected_body_length = int(descriptor.get("b
         ody_length") or 0)
    159 +        if body_sha256 != expected_body_sha256:
    160 +            raise ValueError("relay_request_body_dig
         est_conflict")
    161 +        if len(body) != expected_body_length:
    162 +            raise ValueError("relay_request_body_len
         gth_conflict")
    163 +        if len(body) > relay_contract.limit("request
         _body_bytes"):
    164 +            raise ValueError("relay_request_body_lim
         it_exceeded")
    165 +        request_digest = self._request_digest(
    166 +            method,
    167 +            path,
    168 +            query,
    169 +            headers,
    170 +            body_sha256,
    171 +            len(body),
    172 +        )
    173 +        if request_digest != str(descriptor.get("req
         uest_digest") or ""):
    174 +            raise ValueError("relay_request_descript
         or_digest_conflict")
    175 +        relay_activity_log.success(
    176 +            "operation.request.validated",
    177 +            operation_id=operation_id,
    178 +            pairing_id=pairing_id,
    179 +            user_id=user_id,
    180 +            method=method,
    181 +            route=route,
    182 +            retry_policy=retry_policy,
    183 +            body=body,
    184 +            request_digest=request_digest,
    185 +        )
    186 +        return {
    187 +            "operation_id": operation_id,
    188 +            "claimed_revision": int(descriptor.get("
         revision") or 0),
    189 +            "pairing_id": pairing_id,
    190 +            "user_id": user_id,
    191 +            "method": method,
    192 +            "path": path,
    193 +            "route": route,
    194 +            "query": query,
    195 +            "headers": headers,
    196 +            "body": body,
    197 +            "body_sha256": body_sha256,
    198 +            "request_digest": request_digest,
    199 +            "retry_policy": retry_policy,
    200 +        }
    201 +
    202 +    def _request_body(
    203 +        self,
    204 +        descriptor: Mapping[str, Any],
    205 +        operation_id: str,
    206 +    ) -> bytes:
    207 +        body_base64 = descriptor.get("body_base64")
    208 +        body_ref = str(descriptor.get("body_ref") or
          "")
    209 +        if body_base64 is not None and body_ref:
    210 +            raise ValueError("relay_request_body_sou
         rce_conflict")
    211 +        if body_base64 is not None:
    212 +            body = base64.b64decode(str(body_base64)
         , validate=True)
    213 +            relay_activity_log.debug(
    214 +                "operation.request.inline_body.loade
         d",
    215 +                operation_id=operation_id,
    216 +                body=body,
    217 +            )
    218 +            return body
    219 +        if body_ref:
    220 +            response = relay_transport.request_bytes
         (
    221 +                "GET",
    222 +                relay_contract.endpoint("request_blo
         b", blob_id=body_ref),
    223 +                timeout=relay_contract.duration("req
         uest_timeout_seconds"),
    224 +                action="operation.request_blob.downl
         oad",
    225 +            )
    226 +            return bytes(response.content or b"")
    227 +        return b""
    228 +
    229 +    @staticmethod
    230 +    def _request_digest(
    231 +        method: str,
    232 +        path: str,
    233 +        query: Mapping[str, Any],
    234 +        headers: Mapping[str, Any],
    235 +        body_sha256: str,
    236 +        body_length: int,
    237 +    ) -> str:
    238 +        canonical = json.dumps(
    239 +            {
    240 +                "method": str(method).upper(),
    241 +                "path": "/" + str(path).strip().lstr
         ip("/"),
    242 +                "query": dict(query),
    243 +                "headers": {
    244 +                    str(key).lower(): str(value)
    245 +                    for key, value in sorted(
    246 +                        dict(headers).items(),
    247 +                        key=lambda item: str(item[0]
         ).lower(),
    248 +                    )
    249 +                },
    250 +                "body_sha256": str(body_sha256),
    251 +                "body_length": int(body_length),
    252 +            },
    253 +            ensure_ascii=False,
    254 +            allow_nan=False,
    255 +            sort_keys=True,
    256 +            separators=(",", ":"),
    257 +        ).encode("utf-8")
    258 +        return hashlib.sha256(canonical).hexdigest()
    259 +
    260 +    def _post_response(
    261 +        self,
    262 +        request: Mapping[str, Any],
    263 +        response: RpcExecutionResponse,
    264 +        outcome: str,
    265 +    ) -> None:
    266 +        operation_id = str(request["operation_id"])
    267 +        body_digest = hashlib.sha256(response.body).
         hexdigest()
    268 +        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {
    269 +            "operation_id": operation_id,
    270 +            "claimed_revision": int(request["claimed
         _revision"]),
    271 +            "outcome": str(outcome),
    272 +            "status": int(response.status_code),
    273 +            "headers": rpc_execution_kernel.filtered
         _headers(
    274 +                response.headers,
    275 +                "response",
    276 +            ),
    277 +            "body_sha256": body_digest,
    278 +            "body_length": len(response.body),
    279 +        }
    280 +        if len(response.body) <= relay_contract.limi
         t("inline_body_bytes"):
    281 +            payload["body_base64"] = base64.b64encod
         e(response.body).decode("ascii")
    282 +        else:
    283 +            payload["body_ref"] = self._upload_respo
         nse_blob(
    284 +                operation_id,
    285 +                response.body,
    286 +                body_digest,
    287 +            )
    288 +        relay_transport.request_json(
    289 +            "POST",
    290 +            relay_contract.endpoint(
    291 +                "operation_result",
    292 +                operation_id=operation_id,
    293 +            ),
    294 +            payload,
    295 +            timeout=relay_contract.duration("request
         _timeout_seconds"),
    296 +            action="operation.result.submit",
    297 +        )
    298 +        relay_activity_log.success(
    299 +            "operation.result.submitted",
    300 +            operation_id=operation_id,
    301 +            outcome=outcome,
    302 +            status=response.status_code,
    303 +            body=response.body,
    304 +            body_sha256=body_digest,
    305 +        )
    306 +
    307 +    def _post_execution_unknown(
    308 +        self,
    309 +        request: Mapping[str, Any],
    310 +        code: str,
    311 +    ) -> None:
    312 +        operation_id = str(request["operation_id"])
    313 +        relay_transport.request_json(
    314 +            "POST",
    315 +            relay_contract.endpoint(
    316 +                "operation_result",
    317 +                operation_id=operation_id,
    318 +            ),
    319 +            {
    320 +                "operation_id": operation_id,
    321 +                "claimed_revision": int(request["cla
         imed_revision"]),
    322 +                "outcome": RELAY_EXECUTION_UNKNOWN,
    323 +                "error": {"code": str(code)},
    324 +                "body_sha256": hashlib.sha256(b"").h
         exdigest(),
    325 +                "body_length": 0,
    326 +                "body_base64": "",
    327 +            },
    328 +            action="operation.result.unknown",
    329 +        )
    330 +
    331 +    def _upload_response_blob(
    332 +        self,
    333 +        operation_id: str,
    334 +        body: bytes,
    335 +        body_digest: str,
    336 +    ) -> str:
    337 +        allocation = relay_transport.request_json(
    338 +            "POST",
    339 +            relay_contract.endpoint(
    340 +                "response_blob_allocate",
    341 +                operation_id=operation_id,
    342 +            ),
    343 +            {
    344 +                "operation_id": operation_id,
    345 +                "direction": "response",
    346 +                "expected_sha256": body_digest,
    347 +                "expected_length": len(body),
    348 +            },
    349 +            action="operation.response_blob.allocate
         ",
    350 +        )
    351 +        blob = allocation.get("blob") if isinstance(
         allocation.get("blob"), dict) else allocation
    352 +        blob_id = str(blob.get("blob_id") or "")
    353 +        if not blob_id:
    354 +            raise RuntimeError("relay_response_blob_
         id_missing")
    355 +        chunk_size = relay_contract.limit("blob_chun
         k_bytes")
    356 +        for chunk_index, offset in enumerate(range(0
         , len(body), chunk_size)):
    357 +            chunk = body[offset : offset + chunk_siz
         e]
    358 +            relay_transport.request_bytes(
    359 +                "PUT",
    360 +                relay_contract.endpoint(
    361 +                    "response_blob_chunk",
    362 +                    blob_id=blob_id,
    363 +                    chunk_index=chunk_index,
    364 +                ),
    365 +                body=chunk,
    366 +                action="operation.response_blob.chun
         k",
    367 +            )
    368 +        relay_transport.request_json(
    369 +            "POST",
    370 +            relay_contract.endpoint("response_blob_f
         inalize", blob_id=blob_id),
    371 +            {
    372 +                "blob_id": blob_id,
    373 +                "expected_sha256": body_digest,
    374 +                "expected_length": len(body),
    375 +            },
    376 +            action="operation.response_blob.finalize
         ",
    377 +        )
    378 +        return blob_id

• 终端卡顿点已经确认：旧流程每次获取窗口快照都会同步截取所有
  在线终端，并把 PNG 再转 Base64 塞进 JSON；这会同时放大屏幕
  抓取、编码、JSON 序列化和中转载荷。现有新缓存补丁完整，我正
  在把调用层改成“查看者需求租约 + 摘要资源描述 + 独立原始 PNG
  获取”，并把变更事件接入 Relay 出站上报。

• 终端路径现已从请求线程里移除了批量截屏：窗口快照只登记可见
  窗口需求并立即返回已有资源元数据，实际截屏由受租约约束的总
  线任务合并执行；相同 PNG 摘要不增加版本、不发事件，也不会重
  新上传。旧摘要资源短期保留，支持 ETag/304 和正在途中的远端
  读取。接下来只做静态一致性审查和交接文档更新。

     54 +## Terminal performance correction
     55 +
     56 +The primary slowdown was architectural: every `ui/te
         rminal/windows` snapshot
     57 +captured every online terminal, PNG-encoded each cap
         ture, Base64-expanded it,
     58 +and copied all images into the JSON response. The po
         lling interval repeated the
     59 +work even when a window was unchanged.
     60 +
     61 +The Python domain now:
     62 +
     63 +- returns metadata, a stable state digest, screensho
         t revision, and resource
     64 +  descriptors without Base64 image bytes;
     65 +- records a short viewer-demand lease for explicitly
          visible window IDs;
     66 +- claims capture per window, coalesces concurrent re
         quests, and performs the
     67 +  capture outside the snapshot request thread;
     68 +- uses a non-zero freshness interval and does not in
         crement revision or emit an
     69 +  event when the PNG digest is unchanged;
     70 +- retains bounded immutable digest-addressed PNG res
         ources and serves exact
     71 +  bytes through `ui/terminal/screenshot` with ETag a
         nd 304 semantics;
     72 +- publishes metadata-only `terminal.changed` events
         through ThreadBus, then the
     73 +  outbound Relay device-event endpoint; screenshots
         never enter Mercure.

     12 -- PHP and Python adapters must not duplicate contrac
         t defaults.
     13 -- Each AI changes only its owned source files.
     14 -- Contract mismatches, blockers, endpoint payload cl
         arifications, and completed
     15 -  handoffs are appended here.
     16 -- No compatibility dual-read or dual-write path is i
         ntroduced.
     11 +  currently owned by the completed Python task. PHP
         must read it and must not
     12 +  duplicate fallback endpoints, limits, headers, eve
         nts, policies, or states.
     13 +- The contract digest is lowercase SHA-256 of the ex
         act JSON file bytes. Do not
     14 +  parse and re-encode the file before hashing it.
     15 +- Each AI changes only its owned sources. The Python
          AI changed no PHP/UI/Shell
     16 +  file. The Laravel AI records its status in the Lar
         avel progress document and
     17 +  appends contract questions or completed handoffs h
         ere.
     18 +- V1 is not dual-read or dual-written. Idempotency i
         s implemented at each
     19 +  smallest durable step, never as one umbrella early
          return.
     20
        ⋮
     22
     20 -Pending Python implementation and shared contract pu
         blication.
     23 +Python implementation is complete statically. Larave
         l and UI implementation is
     24 +still owned by the parallel AI.
     25 +
     26 +### Signed device requests
     27 +
     28 +All coordinator JSON bodies are UTF-8 JSON with sort
         ed keys and compact
     29 +separators. Pycore signs the exact transmitted bytes
          with Ed25519. Base64url key
     30 +and signature values omit padding. The canonical inp
         ut is UTF-8 text containing
     31 +these lines, including empty normalized query when a
         bsent:
     32 +
     33 +```text
     34 +protocol-version
     35 +credential-version
     36 +HTTP-method
     37 +normalized-path
     38 +normalized-sorted-query
     39 +device-id
     40 +timestamp
     41 +nonce
     42 +SHA-256(body-bytes)
     43 +```
     44 +
     45 +Normalization rules:
     46 +
     47 +- method is uppercase;
     48 +- path is one leading slash plus the supplied path w
         ith leading slashes removed;
     49 +- query pairs are sorted by string key then string v
         alue and encoded using
     50 +  standard URL form encoding (`urllib.parse.urlencod
         e`, spaces become `+`);
     51 +- timestamp is Unix seconds;
     52 +- nonce is a random URL-safe string and is single-us
         e per credential version;
     53 +- body digest is lowercase hexadecimal SHA-256, incl
         uding the empty body;
     54 +- the exact header names come from `signature_profil
         e.headers`.
     55 +
     56 +Enrollment creation is signed by the proposed device
          key before a credential
     57 +exists. Its credential-version header is the key ver
         sion and it has no
     58 +credential-ID header. Laravel must verify it against
          the public key in the
     59 +enrollment payload. Later enrollment status requests
          are verified against the
     60 +stored proposed key. Credentialed calls require the
         credential-ID header.
     61 +
     62 +Laravel validation steps remain independent: protoco
         l, device/enrollment,
     63 +credential state/version, timestamp window, content
         digest, Ed25519 signature,
     64 +then atomic nonce insertion. A failure in one step m
         ust not skip repair or
     65 +validation of another step.
     66 +
     67 +### Enrollment and runtime endpoints
     68 +
     69 +Endpoint templates are authoritative in the shared c
         ontract.
     70 +
     71 +- Enrollment create request:
     72 +  `{"device":{"device_id","label","platform","public
         _key","key_algorithm":"ed25519","key_version","contr
         act_digest","capability_digest","capabilities"}}`.
     73 +- Enrollment create/status response data contains
     74 +  `enrollment:{enrollment_id,state,claim_code,expire
         s_at}`. A claimed response
     75 +  also contains `credential:{credential_id,credentia
         l_version}` and may contain
     76 +  the hub descriptor.
     77 +- Heartbeat request contains `device_id`, `contract_
         digest`, and `capabilities`.
     78 +- Hub authorization request contains `device_id` and
          `contract_digest`.
     79 +- Hub response data contains
     80 +  `hub:{url,topic,subscriber_token,expires_in_second
         s}`. The token is a
     81 +  short-lived private subscriber token and must neve
         r enter URLs or logs.
     82 +- The device subscribes to only the contract-rendere
         d `device_wake` topic and
     83 +  handles `relay.operation.available` and `relay.cre
         dential.revoked`.
     84 +- Claim request contains `device_id`, stable process
          `lease_owner`, bounded
     85 +  `limit`, and `contract_digest`. Response data cont
         ains `operations:[]`.
     86 +- Device event request contains `device_id`, `event_
         type`, `revision`, and
     87 +  metadata-only `payload`. For `terminal.changed`, L
         aravel fans out a compact
     88 +  pairing-status notification through its transactio
         nal outbox; it never puts
     89 +  screenshot bytes in Mercure.
     90 +
     91 +### Claimed operation descriptor
     92 +
     93 +Each claim item contains:
     94 +
     95 +```text
     96 +operation_id, revision, state, pairing_id, user_id,
     97 +method, path, query, headers,
     98 +body_present, body_base64 OR body_ref,
     99 +body_sha256, body_length, request_digest
    100 +```
    101 +
    102 +`body_base64` is standard padded Base64. `body_prese
         nt=false` is distinct from
    103 +an explicitly present empty body. A present empty bo
         dy uses empty Base64,
    104 +length 0, and the SHA-256 of empty bytes. `body_ref`
          is fetched as exact raw
    105 +bytes from the contract request-blob endpoint.
    106 +
    107 +Pycore recalculates the request digest over compact,
          key-sorted UTF-8 JSON:
    108 +
    109 +```json
    110 +{
    111 +  "method": "UPPERCASE",
    112 +  "path": "/one-leading-slash/path",
    113 +  "query": {},
    114 +  "headers": {"allowed-lowercase-name": "string valu
         e"},
    115 +  "body_present": true,
    116 +  "body_sha256": "lowercase hex",
    117 +  "body_length": 0
    118 +}
    119 +```
    120 +
    121 +Only contract-allowed headers participate. Operation
         /device/user/pairing
    122 +ownership is server-derived. A different digest unde
         r an existing operation ID
    123 +is rejected. `cancel_requested`/`canceled` and `expi
         red` claim descriptors are
    124 +acknowledged without local execution.
    125 +
    126 +### Result submission and blobs
    127 +
    128 +Execution result payload fields are:
    129 +
    130 +```text
    131 +operation_id, claimed_revision, outcome, status, hea
         ders,
    132 +body_present, body_sha256, body_length, result_diges
         t,
    133 +body_base64 OR body_ref
    134 +```
    135 +
    136 +`status` and `result_digest` are omitted for non-exe
         cution outcomes that have no
    137 +HTTP response. Outcomes are contract-owned. Headers
         are lower-case and filtered
    138 +by the response allowlist. No-body is distinct from
         an empty body.
    139 +
    140 +`result_digest` is SHA-256 of compact, key-sorted UT
         F-8 JSON containing status,
    141 +lower-case sorted headers, body presence, body SHA-2
         56, and body length. Laravel
    142 +must use the complete result plus outcome for duplic
         ate-result conflict checks;
    143 +body digest alone is insufficient.
    144 +
    145 +Responses at or below `inline_body_bytes` use standa
         rd Base64. Larger responses
    146 +use these independently idempotent steps:
    147 +
    148 +1. allocate with `operation_id`, direction `response
         `, expected SHA-256, and
    149 +   expected length; return `blob:{blob_id,...}` or a
          root `blob_id`;
    150 +2. PUT immutable raw chunks by `(blob_id, chunk_inde
         x)` using the contract chunk
    151 +   size;
    152 +3. finalize with blob ID, expected SHA-256, and expe
         cted length;
    153 +4. submit the stable operation result referencing `b
         ody_ref`.
    154 +
    155 +Each repeated allocation/chunk/finalize/result with
         identical identity and
    156 +bytes resolves the existing step. Conflicting bytes
         or metadata return conflict.
    157 +
    158 +### Route and execution policy
    159 +
    160 +Pycore applies the local contract again after Larave
         l validation. Unclassified
    161 +`ui/*`, streaming events, open/reveal/path-picker ac
         tions, CodeSync workspace
    162 +operations, and Laravel endpoint-control routes are
         denied. Terminal routes are
    163 +classified individually. Safe recovery is allowed on
         ly for `read` and genuine
    164 +`idempotent_write`; keyboard, mouse, activation, inp
         ut, Enter, history, and
    165 +scroll are `at_most_once_action` and become `executi
         on_unknown` after an
    166 +ambiguous crash.
    167 +
    168 +### Terminal UI contract
    169
    170 +- `POST ui/terminal/windows` accepts `viewer_id` and
    171 +  `visible_window_ids:string[]`. It returns window m
         etadata, `state_revision`,
    172 +  `screenshot_revision`, and optional `window.screen
         shot_resource`. It never
    173 +  returns `window.screenshot` Base64.
    174 +- `POST ui/terminal/viewer_demand` renews the same b
         ounded viewer/window lease.
    175 +- A screenshot descriptor contains MIME, digest, ETa
         g, size, capture timestamp,
    176 +  revision, and `resource:{route,window_id,digest}`.
    177 +- `GET ui/terminal/screenshot?window_id=...&digest=.
         ..` returns raw `image/png`,
    178 +  immutable cache headers, and ETag; matching `If-No
         ne-Match` returns 304.
    179 +- Capture is asynchronous for snapshots, so the firs
         t snapshot may not yet have
    180 +  a descriptor. `terminal.changed` or bounded snapsh
         ot reconciliation supplies
    181 +  it. UI must request resources only for visible win
         dows/active preview and
    182 +  isolate its clock from the terminal tree.
    183 +

    306 +    @serialized_method

› 查看畜生CODEX另一个畜生CODEX查找的是否合理，如果合理调整代
  码并继续推进进度。····确切说：上一条回答没有联网查官方文
  档，主要依据本地设计、现有代码和分布式
    系统推演。若把它说成“官方结论”，就是不准确。现在核对官方
  资料后，结论需要
    分层修正。

    ### 官方资料能够直接确认的部分

    1. Mercure 只做通知，业务状态另有真相源是正确的

       Mercure 官方规范明确说 Hub
       可能丢弃历史事件，检测到丢失后订阅者应重新获取原始资
  源。因此“Mercure
       唤醒/通知，PostgreSQL 保存权威状态”符合官方语义。
  Mercure 规范
       (https://mercure.rocks/spec)

    2. 一个连接订阅多个私有 Topic 是成立的

       官方规范允许一个 subscriber 建立多个 subscription；私
  有事件必须由
       token 的 subscribe 权限和 topic 匹配授权。因此设计中
  的单一复用 SSE
       连接、topic-scoped token 方向正确。Mercure 私有订阅规
  则
       (https://mercure.rocks/spec)

    3. PostgreSQL 行锁只能保护 claim 事务，不能保护事务外执
  行

       PostgreSQL 明确规定行锁在事务结束时释放。PostgreSQL
  Explicit Locking

  (https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/explicit-locking.html)

       所以文档中的短事务 claim 可以防止两个 Laravel worker
  同时领取，但事务
       提交后，不能防止旧 Pycore 卡住、lease 过期、新 Pycore
  再领取。这个不
       是猜测，是由 PostgreSQL 锁语义直接推出的。

    4. 长时间租约需要续期和条件更新

       AWS 官方分布式锁文档明确使用 heartbeat 延长 lease，并
  用 conditional
       write 保证竞争者只有一个成功。AWS DynamoDB
  Distributed Locking


  (https://docs.aws.amazon.com/amazondynamodb/latest/develop
  erguide/BestPractices_DistributedLocking.html)

       因此 Relay 对长任务至少需要：
        - lease renewal；
        - claim generation/epoch；
        - 基于当前 revision/epoch 的条件状态更新。

    5. HTTP 签名必须精确定义规范化规则

       RFC 9421 明确要求应用 profile 定义被签名组件以及确定
  性的
       canonicalization。设计只写
       normalized-path、normalized-sorted-query，但没有定义
  重复参数、百分号
       编码、空值、Unicode、代理重写等规则，因此确实不完整。
  RFC 9421
       (https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9421.html)

    ### 需要修正我上一条中的过度断言

    1. “Enrollment 创建时必须 PoP”不是该项目必须遵守的官方规
  定

       RFC 8628 是相似的设备授权流程，不是这个自定义 Relay
  协议的强制标准。
       它明确支持：
        - 用户看到的短码；
        - 设备持有、不展示的高熵 device code；
        - 短码尝试限速；
        - 明确告知用户正在授权设备。

       这些值得采用。RFC 8628 安全要求
       (https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8628.html)

       RFC 9449
       则证明：如果设备凭证要绑定公钥，就应要求使用者证明持
  有对应私钥。RFC
       9449 DPoP (https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc9449/)

       但这不等于 enrollment 首次创建必须签名。如果
  enrollment ID 是高熵秘
       密，最终 credential 又严格绑定公钥，流程也能安全。准
  确结论是文档没写
       清楚绑定方式，不是已经证明存在漏洞。

    2. “必须建 blob chunk 数据库表”说得过头

       官方没有这种要求。可以使用数据库 chunk 表，也可以使用
  不可变文件加持久
       化 manifest。真正必要的是能够可靠证明：
        - 每个 index 唯一；
        - chunk digest 正确；
        - index 连续；
        - finalization 并发安全。

       所以应要求“持久化 chunk manifest”，不能武断限定必须建
  表。

    3. Fencing token 不能阻止已经发往键鼠设备的物理动作

       它只能阻止过期 claimant 更新服务器状态。准确方案应
  是：
        - claimant 携带 claim epoch；
        - Pycore 在执行前请求 execution_started；
        - Laravel 用 epoch/revision 条件更新；
        - 只有成功确认 execution_started 后才能执行；
        - at_most_once_action 一旦进入该状态，绝不自动重新领
  取；
        - crash 后进入 execution_unknown。

       这才能满足 at-most-once；不能声称 fencing 能撤回已经
  执行的鼠标或键盘
       动作。

    ### 当前工作区可以确定的问题

    设计要求 contract 包含 legal transitions、lease/token/
  retention、per-
    owner limits、permission、payload、timeout 和 Mercure
  profile，参见
    docs_fix/DESIGN_20260823_PYCORE_REMOTE_RELAY_V2.md:111。

    但当前 config/pycore_relay_contract.json:1：

    - 没有 legal transition 表；
    - 没有 lease/token/retention duration；
    - 没有 per-owner storage limit；
    - route 没有 permission、payload、timeout；
    - 没有完整 Mercure profile；
    - 末尾用 ui/ 前缀放行所有 UI GET/POST，config/
      pycore_relay_contract.json:243，与“未显式分类默认拒
  绝”冲突。

    最终确切结论：**设计的 Mercure/PostgreSQL/outbound-only
  主架构有官方语义
    支持，不是瞎猜；但“协议已经完整、可以冻结”没有依据。当前
  现有 contract 未
    落实设计自己的约束。**其余如 PoP、chunk 表、灰度切换属于


• 这份复核大体合理，而且指出了当前实现中两个真实缺口：本地
  ledger 只能约束 Pycore 重放，不能替代 Laravel 租约
  的 execution_started + claim_epoch + lease renewal，同时保
  持 at-most-once 动作只能在服务端确认执行权后开始。

• Searching the web

• Searched the web for https://mercure.rocks/spec

◦ Searching the web

• Searched the web for site:mercure.rocks/spec history lost
  original resource subscriber multiple subscriptions private
  updates ...

• Explored
  └ Read DESIGN_20260823_PYCORE_REMOTE_RELAY_V2.md,
         relay_processor.py, pycore_relay_contract.json

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

• 官方资料核对后的结论与复核一致：PostgreSQL 行锁在事务结束后
  不再保护外部 Pycore 执行；Mercure 事件历史不能当业务真相；
  RFC 9421 也要求自定义 profile 把规范化规则写死。需要修正的
  不是主架构，而是执行权协议。现在我会把它落实为：claim 返回
  claim_epoch，Pycore 在任何 handler（尤其键鼠动作）前必须条
  件确认 execution_started，长执行独立续租，所有结果携带
  epoch/owner/revision；fencing 只阻止陈旧结果写回，不声称能
  撤销已经发生的物理动作。
